Motivation: 澄清Sophisticated Inference在主动推理中的核心计算角色，揭示其优势的根源（闭环控制 vs. 树搜索），并验证epistemic-prior变分自由能框架能否表征这种闭环结构。

Method: 在随机基准任务Reactivity Maze中，比较三种变分目标（共享相同状态-行动后验族）、行动-状态因子化主动推理目标、Sophisticated Inference以及标准期望自由能规划，通过分离epistemic激励与内视界闭环控制进行评估。

Result: 缺乏epistemic成分的方法无法主动寻求信息；阻止未来行动依赖未来状态的方法无法将信息转化为可靠的目地达成。仅Sophisticated Inference和全联合epistemic-prior主动推理（同时具备epistemic驱动和闭环推理）能成功解决环境。

Conclusion: Sophisticated Inference的优势并非树搜索所特有，而是源于主动推理的闭环形式（后验中未来行动依赖未来状态），这种形式可在epistemic-prior变分推理中表征。

Abstract: Sophisticated Inference is a variant of active inference often associated with recursive belief modeling and tree search. We argue that its central computational role is simpler: within a planning horizon, it makes active inference closed-loop by allowing future actions to depend on future states and observations. This closed-loop structure can be represented in the epistemic-prior variational free energy framework. Epistemic priors supply the active-inference objective, while a joint posterior over future states and actions supplies the state-contingent control structure. We evaluate this decomposition in the Reactivity Maze, a stochastic benchmark designed to separate epistemic incentive from inner-horizon closed-loop control. The comparison includes three variational objectives with the same state-action posterior family, an action-state factorized active inference objective, Sophisticated Inference, and standard Expected Free Energy planning. The results show that neither ingredient is sufficient on its own. Methods without an epistemic component do not seek information, while methods that prevent future actions from depending on future states cannot turn information into reliable goal-reaching. By contrast, both Sophisticated Inference and full-joint epistemic-prior active inference solve the environment by combining epistemic drive with closed-loop inference. These results show that the advantage associated with Sophisticated Inference need not be specific to tree search itself. It arises from the closed-loop form of active inference, and this form can be represented in epistemic-prior variational inference when the posterior keeps future actions dependent on future states.

</details>


### [76] [Knowledge-Centric Self-Improvement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19592)
*Xuefei Julie Wang,Lauren Hyoseo Yoon,Chengrui Qu,Amanda Zichang Wang,Atharva Sehgal,Eric Mazumdar,Yisong Yue*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出知识中心型自我改进（Knowledge-Centric Self-Improvement）范式，替代传统的智能体中心型自我改进。通过让可丢弃的通用智能体在执行任务后将基于证据的洞察贡献到可持久化的知识库中，实现知识的跨任务、跨模型迁移。在抽象推理、编程和终端基准测试中，该方法相比智能体中心基线提升了解决率并降低了成本，且蒸馏后的知识可迁移至未见任务和不同LLM家族。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统智能体中心型自我改进将智能体自身作为改进对象（优化提示词、工作流等），导致改进成果与特定智能体设计、任务分布或适应运行紧密绑定，维护成本高且难以迁移。作者希望探索一种互补范式，使改进成果固化在知识中而非智能体中，从而提升可检查性、可迁移性和可移植性。

Method: 提出知识中心型自我改进协议：(1) 智能体执行任务；(2) 通过任务级和跨任务论坛贡献基于证据的洞察；(3) 进行知识蒸馏，将洞察整合到持久化的知识库中。智能体保持通用且可丢弃，知识库作为持续改进的持久对象。

Result: 在抽象推理、编程和终端基准测试中，该协议相比智能体中心基线提升了解决率并降低了成本。蒸馏后的知识能够迁移到未见任务和不同LLM家族（如从Llama迁移到GPT），表明改进并非LLM或运行特定行为。

Conclusion: 知识中心型自我改进是一种可行的新范式，进步可以主要由精心策划的持久知识驱动，支持自我改进智能体系统的新视角。

Abstract: Self-improving AI systems typically treat the agent as the object that improves, by optimizing prompts, workflows, harnesses, or even the agent's own code. This agent-centric view can make improvements expensive to maintain and difficult to transfer, because gains become tied to a particular agent design, task distribution, or adaptation run. We study a complementary paradigm: knowledge-centric self-improvement, in which agents remain generic and disposable while the persistent object is a curated knowledge base that agents can leverage for future tasks. We conduct controlled case studies to operationalize this idea via a simple protocol. Agents attempt one task, then contribute evidence-grounded insights to a shared knowledge base via task-level and cross-task forums, followed by knowledge distillation. Because self-improvement is contained in the knowledge rather than the agent, improvement can be more inspectable, transferable, and portable. Across abstract reasoning, coding, and terminal benchmarks, this protocol improves solve rates while reducing dollar cost relative to agent-centric baselines. The resulting distilled knowledge also transfers to held-out tasks and across LLM families, indicating that the improvement is not merely an LLM- or run-specific behavior. These results support a new view of self-improving agentic systems: progress can be driven primarily by the curated persistent knowledge. Code is available at https://github.com/recursive-knowledge/KSI.

</details>


### [77] [Edge Intelligence in Civil Aviation: Paradigms, Techniques, and Applications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19676)
*Wenbin Li,Zhongtian Liao,Bolin Liu,Yongjie Zhou,Jingling Wu,Xiaoyong Lin,Jing Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文综述了面向民用航空的边缘智能技术，通过将AI推理和学习推向网络边缘，解决云中心部署在时延、离线能力、隐私和主权方面的挑战，并展望了其在航空全生命周期的应用与未来研究方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 民用航空是安全关键领域，其运营产生海量异构边缘数据，但云端大模型部署存在高任务延迟、通信拒止环境下缺乏离线能力、集中化敏感数据引发隐私和主权风险等问题，亟需将AI推向边缘。

Method: 本文采用综述方法，首先阐述边缘AI的运营动机，然后回顾边缘推理和边缘学习的最新技术，接着介绍组织计算范式及在民航环境中的配置，最后描述新兴应用和未来研究趋势。

Result: 提出精细化的边缘解决方案能够补充云基础设施，为民航全生命周期提供低延迟、隐私保护和弹性的AI服务。

Conclusion: 边缘智能通过压缩、协同推理和分割学习等技术，可有效降低延迟、带宽和隐私暴露风险，并在断连时保持可用性，是云中心方案的重要补充。

Abstract: Civil aviation is safety critical and its operations, from flight decks and towers to ramps and maintenance, generate massive, heterogeneous data at the network edge. Yet cloud centric deployment of large Artificial Intelligence (AI) models often produces high task latency, lacks offline capability in communication denied environments, and requires centralizing sensitive data, raising privacy and sovereignty risks. Edge AI moves perception, prediction, and decision logic closer to the data producers via compression, collaborative inference, and split learning, thereby reducing latency, bandwidth, and exposure while enabling graceful operation during disconnections. This paper provides a panoramic view and a common understanding of edge intelligence tailored to civil aviation. We firstly articulate the operational motivations for edge AI, and then review recent techniques for edge inference and edge learning. We then introduce the organizational computing paradigms and the respective configurations in civil aviation environments; finally, we describe the emerging applications and the future research trends of edge intelligence in civil aviation. We argue that a refined edge solution can complement cloud foundations to deliver low latency, privacy preserving, and resilient AI services across the civil aviation lifecycle.

</details>


### [78] [Symbol and Footprint Database for Electronic Components by Agentic Recognition and Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19767)
*Yichen Shi,Yuzhi Liu,Zhuofu Tao,Li Huang,Yuhao Gao,Ting-Jung Lin,Lei Hel*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了SFgen，一种基于多模态大语言模型的电子元器件符号与封装自动化识别与生成流程，在符号生成上达到86%准确率，封装生成上达到80%准确率，并基于该方法构建了包含1000个元器件的SFnet数据库，为PCB设计的自动生成奠定基础。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统PCB设计中，工程师手工创建元器件符号和封装并设计原理图，耗时且易出错，亟需自动化方法提升效率与准确性。

Method: 利用多模态大语言模型(MLLMs)构建SFgen智能体流程，实现电子元器件符号(symbol)和封装(footprint)的自动识别与生成。

Result: 符号生成准确率达86%，封装生成准确率达80%；基于SFgen构建的SFnet数据库已包含1000个元器件且持续扩展。

Conclusion: SFgen方法有效实现了PCB元器件符号与封装的自动化生成，SFnet数据库为PCB设计的全自动生成提供了基础支撑。

Abstract: A rich and recognizable component library is the cornerstone of printed circuit board (PCB) design and generation. Traditionally, engineers manually create symbols and footprints and design PCB schematics, which is time-consuming and error-prone. Leveraging multimodal large language models (MLLMs), we develop SFgen, an agentic recognition and generation flow of symbol and footprint for electronic components. SFgen achieves 86% accuracy for symbol generation and 80% accuracy for footprint generation. We use the SFgen method to create SFnet, a database of symbols and footprints. It now has 1000 components and is expanding constantly, which lays the foundation for automatic generation of PCB designs.

</details>


### [79] [Silent Failures in Multimodal Agentic Search:A Diagnostic Taxonomy and Cross-Judge Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19793)
*Zhengxian Wu,Junjie Gao,Kai Yang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了多模态智能搜索系统中隐藏的可靠性问题（称为"静默失败"），提出了六类故障分类法，并构建了轨迹级诊断流水线。实验表明，表面准确率会高估真实的轨迹级正确性，且静默失败具有能力依赖性，往往会发生转移而非消失。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有评估主要关注最终答案的准确性，可能遗漏搜索轨迹中的故障。需要系统性地识别和分类这些隐藏的可靠性问题。

Method: 提出六类别分类法（模态捷径、幻影基础、错误证据-正确答案、过度检索洗白、跨模态矛盾、来源幻觉），构建统一ReAct框架下的轨迹级诊断流水线，结合跨判断验证、空白图像压力测试和工具消融实验进行分析。

Result: 表面准确率一致性地高估了真实的轨迹级正确性；静默失败具有能力依赖性，且往往发生转移而非消失。

Conclusion: 需要更全面的轨迹级评估来揭示多模态智能搜索系统中的隐藏故障，仅关注最终答案准确性是不够的。

Abstract: Multimodal agentic search systems increasingly rely on external tools to answer knowledge-intensive visual questions. However, existing evaluations mainly focus on final-answer accuracy and may miss failures in the search trajectory. In this work, we study such hidden reliability issues as silent failures. We introduce a six-category taxonomy covering modality shortcuts, phantom grounding, wrong-evidence-right-answer cases, over-retrieval laundering, cross-modal contradiction, and provenance hallucination. Based on this taxonomy, we build a trajectory-level diagnostic pipeline that evaluates both answer correctness and evidence-grounding quality under a unified ReAct-style scaffold. Experiments on MMSearch-Plus trajectories across four frontier multimodal models show that surface accuracy consistently overestimates true trajectory-level correctness. We further use cross-judge validation, blank-image stress tests, and tool ablations to show that silent failures are capability-dependent and often shift rather than disappear. Home-page: https://github.com/DingWu1021/silent-failures-multimodal-agentic-search

</details>


### [80] [Rewarding Better Thinking for LLM Preference Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19824)
*Xubo Liu,Wenya Guo,Ruxue Yan,Xinying Qian,Ying Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出Thinking Checklist Reward (TCR)，一种基于过程奖励的RL偏好对齐方法，通过将偏好对转化为样本特定的思维检查清单来评估推理轨迹，并采用EMA残差公式分离补充性思维盈余，在三类模型家族的五个模型上显著提升对齐性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有偏好对齐中的RL方法使用结果级代理奖励，仅评估最终响应，对推理轨迹的指导有限，导致信用分配粗糙且轨迹级偏好未充分指定。

Method: TCR将偏好对转化为样本特定的思维检查清单，用于评估推理轨迹是否满足偏好隐含的考量；引入EMA残差公式，分离出结果奖励无法预测的补充性思维盈余，减少与结果级监督的重叠。

Result: 在三类模型家族的五个模型上，TCR在多个基准测试中一致提升了对齐性能；消融实验验证了EMA残差公式和样本特定检查清单监督的重要性。

Conclusion: TCR通过过程级奖励有效改善了偏好对齐中对推理轨迹的指导，解决了结果级奖励的信用分配粗粒度问题。

Abstract: LLM preference alignment aims to optimize models toward human preferences across diverse user instructions. Reinforcement learning has become a major post-training approach for this goal, but existing proxy rewards are often outcome-level, mainly evaluating the final response while providing limited guidance for the reasoning trajectory. This can make credit assignment coarse when multiple responses receive similar final scores, leaving trajectory-level preferences under-specified. To address this limitation, we propose Thinking Checklist Reward (TCR), a process-oriented reward for RL-based preference alignment. TCR converts preference pairs into sample-specific thinking checklists and uses them to evaluate whether the generated reasoning trace addresses the preference-implied considerations. To reduce overlap with outcome-level supervision, TCR further introduces an exponential moving average (EMA) residual formulation to isolate a complementary thinking surplus beyond what is predictable from the outcome reward. Experiments on five models from three model families show that TCR consistently improves alignment performance across diverse benchmarks, with ablations further validating the importance of EMA-based residual formulation and sample-specific checklist supervision.

</details>


### [81] [DocOps: A Verifiable Benchmark for Autonomous Agents in Complex Document Operations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19865)
*Jiazhen Jiang,Boxi Cao,Lingyong Yan,Yaojie Lu,Hongyu Lin,Shuaiqiang Wang,Dawei Yin,Xianpei Han,Le Sun*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: DocOps是一个基于分层分类法的文档操作评估框架，用于系统性评测AI代理在复杂文档任务中的表现。研究发现，即使最先进的模型在处理高耦合、长范围任务时仍存在严重缺陷，并揭示了三种关键失败模式：长期状态跟踪崩溃、浅层语义验证和结构元数据破坏性编辑。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自主代理需要可靠地操作数字文档以实现通用AI助手和自动化工作流，但现有评估体系缺乏对文档操作复杂性和一致性的系统性度量。

Method: 提出DocOps框架，基于分层分类法将文档操作分解为原子维度和逐步升级的工作流复杂度；对闭源和开源模型在不同代理框架下进行系统评估。

Result: 最先进的前沿配置在处理高耦合、长范围任务时仍表现出显著局限性；识别出三种关键失败模式：长期状态跟踪崩溃、浅层语义验证、结构元数据破坏性编辑。

Conclusion: 揭示了代理在维持全局文档一致性方面的能力边界，为未来设计鲁棒、非破坏性的复杂数字生态系统代理提供了方向。

Abstract: As autonomous agents rapidly evolve, their ability to reliably manipulate ubiquitous digital documents has become critical for enabling general-purpose AI assistants and automating complex workspace workflows. In this paper, we introduce DocOps, a deterministically verifiable evaluation framework underpinned by a hierarchical taxonomy that deconstructs document operations inspired by real-world practices into atomic dimensions and escalating workflow complexities. Based on DocOps, we systematically evaluate representative closed- and open-source models across various agentic harnesses, revealing that even the most advanced frontier configurations still exhibit profound limitations when handling highly coupled, long-range tasks. Furthermore, a fine-grained analysis of existing agents' manipulation behaviors uncovers 3 key failure modes: long-term state tracking collapse, shallow semantic verification, and destructive editing of structural metadata. Ultimately, our work exposes the capability boundaries of agents in maintaining global document consistency, shedding light on the future design of robust, non-destructive agents for complex digital ecosystems.

</details>


### [82] [Long-Term Sequential Decision Making under Risk](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19914)
*Irmaan,Mirzanejad,Nadjet Bourdache,Abdel-Illah Mouaddib*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出ERQDP方法，通过秩-分位数代理和动态规划，在有限时域MDP中实现基于根风险目标的近似最优规划，并提供显式认证间隙。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 有限时域MDP在根风险目标下（秩依赖泛函）因非线性和破坏Bellman最优性而难以通过场景树枚举直接优化。

Method: 提出ERQDP方法：(1) 通过精确DP求解秩-分位数代理问题；(2) 在离散化回报网格上通过DP评估PMF以精确计算候选策略的目标值（含显式舍入界）；(3) 在任意循环中精炼代理，报告目标值关于离散化预算的显式上下界（认证）。

Result: 在多个基准测试中，ERQDP返回认证解或显式残差间隙，支持快速风险参数扫描并大幅节省运行时，同时兼容风险规避与风险寻求行为。

Conclusion: ERQDP是首个免枚举、免采样的根风险MDP规划方法，通过DP与离散化技术实现可认证的近似最优解。

Abstract: We study finite-horizon MDP planning under \emph{root-based} (resolute) risk objectives that apply a rank-dependent functional to the distribution of total returns. Such objectives are non-linear in the return distribution and generally break Bellman optimality, so direct optimization by scenario-tree enumeration is intractable. We propose \textbf{ERQDP}, an enumeration-free and sampling-free method that solves a rank--quantile surrogate via exact DP (Dynamic Programming), evaluates candidate policies exactly by DP over return Probability Mass Functions (PMFs) on a discretized return grid (with an explicit rounding bound), and refines the surrogate in an anytime loop that reports an explicit upper--lower gap (certificate) for the target objective up to discretization budgets. Across tested benchmarks, ERQDP returns certified solutions or explicit residual gaps, enables fast risk-parameter sweeps with substantial runtime gains, and supports both risk-averse and risk-seeking behaviors.

</details>


### [83] [MOF-Sleuth: Tool-Grounded Reward Alignment for Explainable Fine-Grained MOF CIF Auditing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19935)
*Yu Liu,Zhiwei Yang,Diandian Guo,Kun Peng,Fangfang Yuan,Cong Cao,Chaozhuo Li,Zhiyuan Ma,Yanbing Liu,Guobin Zhao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: MOF-Sleuth是一个基于强化学习的MOF晶体结构文件（CIF）审计智能体，通过确定性法医实验室提取化学证据，并由推理引擎生成证据驱动的诊断结果，在检测、归因和可解释性上达到最先进水平。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MOF数据库中的CIF文件存在细微化学和结构错误，而当前检测方法（验证器、机器学习模型）只能提供固定的检查项或粗粒度标签，无法给出基于证据的解释；直接让LLM审计则成本高且不可靠，因为化学证据隐式分布在原子位点记录中，需要几何、连接性、占据率和电荷计算等推理。

Method: 提出MOF-Sleuth，包含两个模块：1) 确定性法医实验室（Forensic Lab），计算组成、几何、连接性、占据率、配位和电荷等证据；2) Sleuth推理引擎，利用这些证据生成证据驱动的解释、错误类型和二元判断。通过奖励引导的强化学习，将工具测量结果转化为化学层面的解释级监督信号，不仅奖励最终答案，还奖励引用的化学证据及其支持的诊断。同时提出Chem-GD评估指标，衡量诊断是否基于事实且相关的CIF证据。

Result: 在四个基准测试上，MOF-Sleuth在基于LLM的方法和MOF专用机器学习方法中均达到最先进水平，在检测、归因和基于证据的解释质量上均有提升。

Conclusion: 通过强化学习将化学证据与语言模型解释强耦合，MOF-Sleuth有效解决了细粒度归因和不可靠CIF推理两大挑战，为MOF结构质量审计提供了可解释、可验证的解决方案。

Abstract: Large metal-organic framework (MOF) databases support simulation, screening, and machine learning through crystallographic information files (CIFs). Subtle chemical and structural errors in these inputs can compromise downstream results and hinder manual inspection. LLM advances in computational chemistry offer paths beyond predictive screening toward fine-grained diagnosis with evidence-grounded explanations. However, two challenges remain: (i) limited fine-grained attribution: MOF-specific validators and machine-learning models scale detection but provide fixed checks, readiness scores, or coarse labels rather than evidence-grounded explanations; and (ii) unreliable CIF reasoning: direct LLM auditing is costly and unreliable because chemical evidence is implicit across atom-site records and requires geometric, connectivity, occupancy, and charge calculations. Both stem from weak coupling between chemical evidence and language-model explanation. We introduce MOF-Sleuth, a reinforcement-guided CIF auditing agent with two modules: a deterministic Forensic Lab and a Sleuth reasoning engine. The Lab derives composition, geometry, connectivity, occupancy, coordination, and charge evidence, and Sleuth uses this evidence to produce an evidence-grounded explanation, error types, and a binary decision. Reward-guided reinforcement learning (RL) turns tool measurements into chemical explanation-level supervision, rewarding not only the final answer but also cited chemical evidence and evidence-supported diagnoses. We introduce Chemically Grounded Diagnosis (Chem-GD), a metric that assesses whether a correct diagnosis is explained by factual, relevant CIF-derived evidence. Across four benchmarks, MOF-Sleuth establishes state-of-the-art performance among LLM-based approaches and MOF-specific machine-learning methods, demonstrating gains in detection, attribution, and grounded explanation quality.

</details>


### [84] [SenWorld: A Digital-Twin Simulation for Generating Context-Rich Evaluation Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19949)
*Zenghui Zhou,Xiaoyang Li,Xiaoxuan Qiao,Zhilang Wei,Tianming Lei*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: SenWorld是一个基于物理的、确定性的、事件驱动的数字孪生仿真系统，通过构建带有ground truth的个人数据来评估智能手机个人助手，无需依赖真实用户隐私数据或LLM评判。在北京的16个角色实验中，生成数据在类别分布（JSD 0.070）和日常通信节奏（JSD <0.1）上与真实用户基准高度匹配，并成功发现生产级助手在通话和短信记录上的78个检索错误。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 智能手机个人助手需要基于长期个人数据进行推理，但评估这类系统需要具备已知正确答案的上下文丰富的评估数据，而真实设备数据因隐私敏感难以共享。现有方法依赖后验标注或LLM评判，缺乏可验证的ground truth。

Method: SenWorld采用物理接地、确定性事件溯源的数字孪生仿真方法：从真实地图、天气、节假日和网络数据构建世界；角色在完整一天中生活；所有可观测信号以全系统快照存档；每个评估案例通过指向已有记录的指针标注，而非后验标注或LLM评判。

Result: 在北京16个角色实验中，生成数据与真实用户基准在类别分布上JSD为0.070，通信记录日常节奏JSD低于0.1，但生成记录比真实记录短。角色形成完全互惠对话子图和差异化行为库。在717个评估案例中，暴露了生产级助手78个失败，集中在通话和短信记录，联系人、日程和闹钟无失败。

Conclusion: SenWorld提供了一条隐私安全、可复现、分布可验证的评估数据生成路径，其标签由构造本身固定，无需LLM评判介入。每个失败确认为助手端检索错误。

Abstract: Smartphone personal assistants reason over longitudinal personal data, yet evaluating them requires context-rich evaluation data whose correct answers are known, and real device traces are too privacy-sensitive to share. To address this challenge, we present SenWorld, a physically grounded, deterministic, event-sourced digital-twin simulation that generates such data with ground truth fixed by construction. In SenWorld, personas live through a full day in a world built from real map, weather, holiday, and network data; every observable signal is archived in full-system snapshots; and each evaluation case is labeled by a pointer to an existing record rather than by post-hoc annotation or a large language model (LLM) judge. We evaluate this method with 16 personas in Beijing. The generated data closely matches the held-out real-user benchmark in category distribution (Jensen--Shannon divergence (JSD) 0.070) and in the daily rhythm of communication records (JSD below 0.1), though generated records remain shorter than real ones. Without scripted interaction, personas form a fully reciprocated dialogue subgraph and differentiated behavioral repertoires. Projected into 717 evaluation cases, the generated data exposes 78 failures in a production smartphone assistant, concentrating on call and Short Message Service (SMS) records while contacts, schedules, and alarms never fail. The snapshot pointer confirms each failure as an assistant-side retrieval error, with no LLM judge involved. Overall, SenWorld offers a privacy-safe, reproducible, and distribution-checked path to evaluation data whose labels are fixed by construction.

</details>


### [85] [EvoThink: Evolving Thinking in Large Reasoning Models via Self-Pruning and Aha-Moment Preference Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19962)
*Xinbang Dai,Zheyu Xin,Huikang Hu,Lin Ren,Rihui Jin,Guohui Xiao,Guilin Qi,Kuicai Dong,Zhaocheng Du,Yuyang Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: EvoThink是一个通过自我剪枝训练（SPT）消除冗余验证步骤，并利用Aha-Moment偏好优化（AMPO）从失败推理中学习新路径的框架，在提升推理效率的同时增强了推理能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有大型推理模型（LRMs）存在过度思考问题，即冗余的验证步骤降低了推理效率。而现有的缓解方法（如快慢思考切换、推理轨迹压缩）无法细粒度地区分有益步骤与冗余步骤，可能在追求效率时损害推理能力。因此需要一种既能提升推理效率又不损害甚至增强推理能力的方法。

Method: EvoThink包含两个核心组件：1) Self-Pruning Training (SPT)——一种无监督方法，通过迭代剪枝冗余推理步骤并在精简轨迹上进行自我训练；2) Aha-Moment Preference Optimization (AMPO)——受遗传算法启发，识别有价值的失败推理尝试，合成从错误到正确的"啊哈时刻"数据，并优化模型内化这种推理模式。

Result: 在数学推理和代码生成基准测试上的大量评估表明，EvoThink不仅显著减少了推理时的token使用量（提升效率），同时提升了LRMs的推理能力。

Conclusion: EvoThink通过同时剪枝冗余步骤和探索新推理路径，实现了推理效率与能力的双重提升，证明了精细化的冗余消除与从失败中学习相结合的有效性。

Abstract: Large Reasoning Models (LRMs) often suffer from overthinking due to redundant verification steps. Existing approaches for mitigating overthinking, such as fast-slow thinking switching and reasoning trajectory compression, fail to make a fine-grained distinction between beneficial and redundant steps within the LRM's reasoning process, and may thus impair reasoning capability in their pursuit of efficiency. To simultaneously improve reasoning efficiency and capability, we propose EvoThink, a framework that reduces redundant verification and encourages the exploration of new reasoning paths. EvoThink comprises two key components: Self-Pruning Training (SPT), an unsupervised method that iteratively prunes redundant reasoning steps and self-trains on the concise trajectories; and Aha-Moment Preference Optimization (AMPO), which, inspired by genetic algorithms, identifies valuable failed reasoning attempts, synthesizes from-wrong-to-right aha-moment data, and optimizes the model to internalize this reasoning pattern. Extensive evaluations across mathematical reasoning and code generation benchmarks demonstrate that EvoThink not only substantially reduces inference-time token usage but also improves the reasoning capability of LRMs.

</details>


### [86] [The Giant Hippocampus: From Structural Monoculture to a System of Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19973)
*Jaeho Seol*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文指出当前AI领域将同一架构（Transformer）应用于所有任务的"通用模板"做法与神经科学中不同脑区具有不同结构的发现相矛盾，认为这是一个可测量的结构性错误，并提出异质性拓扑网络（Heterogeneous Topological Network）作为替代方案，主张在训练前根据结构证据指定模块化设计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前AI研究将Transformer作为通用架构应用于所有模态（文本、图像、语音），而神经科学一个世纪的细胞结构学证据表明不同认知功能由不同结构实现。这种"结构误差"比风格差异更根本，作者试图弥合这一鸿沟。

Method: 通过回顾细胞结构学历史（Brodmann到Patch-seq）证明结构-功能对应关系；分析CNN的成功源于结构先验（局部感受野、层次深度）而Transformer丢弃了这一教训；指出"硬件彩票"使Transformer成为路径依赖而非原则选择；通过功能类比论证Transformer实质是海马体的功能类似物而非通用皮层。

Result: 识别出Transformer本质上是海马体的功能类似物，却被错误地当作通用皮层应用于非适配任务（听觉、执行门控、工作记忆），最终提出异质性拓扑网络作为解决方案。

Conclusion: AI架构设计应遵循"训练前指定模块化"的设计准则，利用结构证据作为设计输入，而非从训练后模型行为反向工程；异质性拓扑网络（系统之系统）允许不同模块保留其计算所需的归纳偏置并通过标准化接口通信。

Abstract: AI researchers describe state-of-the-art models as one thing repeated at scale: the Transformer, wired identically for text, pixels, or speech. Neuroscientists describe the cortex as a mosaic - dense Layer 4 in visual cortex for spatial encoding, thick Layers 5/6 in motion cortex for temporal integration - different jobs solved by different structures. This paper argues the gap is a structural error, not a stylistic one, and is measurable. A century of cytoarchitecture, from Brodmann to single-cell Patch-seq, shows distinct cognitive functions are implemented by qualitatively different structures, not by rescaling one template. The convolutional neural network is the field's own proof: local receptive fields and hierarchical depth encoded this prior directly, reaching strong image recognition on far less data than later architectures needed. The paper traces how this lesson was discarded: the "Hardware Lottery" made the Transformer the path of least resistance, not the principled choice, and Mixture-of-Experts, often cited as diversity, in fact partitions parameters among identical experts. A functionalist analysis shows the Transformer is best understood as a functional analog of the hippocampal formation, not a general-purpose cortex - the same mistake as treating cortex as one giant Broca's area, except the field has now standardized on a giant hippocampus, applied to tasks it was never built for: audition, executive gating, working memory. The paper closes with an alternative: a Heterogeneous Topological Network, a System of Systems in which distinct modules keep the inductive bias their computation demands and communicate through standardized interfaces. This is a design discipline for AI architects, not cognitive science: specify modularity before training, using structural evidence as a design input rather than reverse-engineering architecture from a trained model's behavior.

</details>


### [87] [Coordinating from Memory: Graph-Structured Experience Reuse for Multi-Agent Adaptation in Dynamic Manufacturing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19985)
*Chengxiao Dai,Zhanhui Lin,Zhaokun Yan,Youyang Ni,Chenjun Lei,Luyan Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出图结构经验记忆（GSEM）框架，利用图神经网络检索历史协调经验，以加速动态制造环境下多智能体系统的自适应协调，在柔性作业车间调度基准上实现了4.1%-10.0%的完工时间降低和33%-38%的适应时间缩减。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MARL方法在动态制造环境中每次扰动后独立处理协调任务，丢弃了有价值的跨扰动协调经验，导致适应效率低下。

Method: 将历史协调经验编码为异构关系图（包含任务依赖、机器状态、智能体协作模式），当新扰动发生时，利用图神经网络检索结构相似的过往经验，指导策略自适应而非从头学习。

Result: 在三种扰动类型的动态柔性作业车间调度基准上，GSEM相比最强记忆增强基线，完工时间降低4.1%-10.0%，适应时间减少33%-38%；优势在高扰动频率下更显著。

Conclusion: 图结构编码和基于相似性的检索对跨扰动知识迁移至关重要，且学到的协调模式具有良好的跨扰动泛化能力。

Abstract: Dynamic manufacturing environments require multi-agent systems to coordinate effectively under frequent operational disturbances such as machine failures, urgent job arrivals, and processing time variations. Existing multi-agent reinforcement learning approaches treat each disturbance episode independently, discarding valuable coordination experience that could accelerate future adaptation. In this paper, we propose a Graph-Structured Experiential Memory (GSEM) framework for multi-agent coordination in dynamic manufacturing. The framework encodes historical coordination episodes as heterogeneous relational graphs that capture task dependencies, machine states, and inter-agent collaboration patterns. When a new disturbance occurs, a graph neural network-based retrieval mechanism identifies structurally similar past episodes, enabling experience-guided policy adaptation rather than learning from scratch. Experiments on dynamic flexible job-shop scheduling benchmarks with three disturbance types show that GSEM reduces makespan by 4.1%-10.0% and adaptation time by 33%-38% compared to the strongest memory-augmented baseline, with the advantage increasing under higher disturbance frequency. Ablation studies and cross-disturbance transfer experiments further validate the necessity of graph-structured encoding and similarity-based retrieval and demonstrate the cross-disturbance generalizability of learned coordination patterns.

</details>


### [88] [CLARK: Closed-loop Learning for Adaptive Reasoning over Knowledge Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19996)
*Yousef Khan,Luca Gherardini,Marco Maratea,Joel Arrais,Jose Sousa*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: CLARK是一个融合知识图谱、符号规则挖掘和概率推理的框架，通过LP^MLN形式化方法实现自适应分类，在医疗数据集上提升了分类性能和泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统机器学习模型在数据分布变化时性能下降，且难以整合先验知识，缺乏对不确定性和演化信息的处理能力。

Method: CLARK框架以CACTUS知识图谱为起点，将其转换为LP^MLN程序，通过符号规则挖掘器迭代提出候选规则，并利用概率权重学习进行校准，实现不确定性推理和底层图结构的优化。

Result: 在两个医疗数据集上的评估表明，CLARK提升了分类性能，并实现了更可泛化的推理。

Conclusion: CLARK提供了一种构建自适应、可解释、知识驱动的分类模型的原则性方法。

Abstract: Machine Learning models are widely used for automating classification tasks by extracting statistical patterns from data. However, their performance deteriorates if the data distribution changes, making them ill-suited to handle uncertain and evolving information. Moreover, they provide limited support for integrating prior knowledge. To address these limitations, we present CLARK (Closed-loop Learning for Adaptive Reasoning over Knowledge Graphs), a framework that integrates knowledge graphs, symbolic rule mining, and probabilistic reasoning under the Logic Programs with Markov Logic Networks (LP$^{\text{MLN}}$) formalism. Starting from CACTUS-derived KGs, CLARK translates graph structure into an LP$^{\text{MLN}}$ program and iteratively enriches it with candidate rules proposed by symbolic learners. These rules are calibrated through probabilistic weight learning, enabling reasoning under uncertainty and refinement of the underlying graph structure. We evaluate CLARK on two medical datasets, analysing both rule quality and downstream classification performance. Results demonstrate that CLARK leads to improved classification performance and more generalisable inference. Overall, CLARK provides a principled approach to constructing adaptive, interpretable, knowledge-driven models for classification.

</details>


### [89] [Safe Remediation as Risk-Constrained Intervention Decision in Microservice Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20005)
*Chengxiao Dai,Zhaokun Yan,Chenjun Lei,Qiao Li,Luyan Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于约束马尔可夫决策过程（CMDP）的自动化修复系统安全决策框架，通过风险约束、三维风险分解和上下文自适应的人机协同门控机制，在降低错误修复率的同时提升修复成功率并减少值班升级负担。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自动化修复系统以生成动作为目标，忽视"何时不应干预"的安全决策，导致错误修复的成本往往高于不采取任何行动的成本，安全性仅靠人工审批保障。

Method: (i) 将安全修复重新定义为风险约束的干预决策问题，建模为CMDP，在最大化修复成功率的同时约束错误修复率(FRR)；(ii) 提出三维风险分解（爆炸半径、可逆性、认知不确定性）提供可解释的安全界面；(iii) 设计上下文自适应的人机协同门控机制，将升级从二元保险转变为带宽感知的控制层。策略从历史事件日志中离线学习。

Result: 在Train Ticket微服务基准测试（Chaos Mesh故障注入+RCAEval故障分类）上，相比强基线runbook方法，FRR降低39%，修复成功率提升2.5个百分点，值班升级负担相比固定阈值变体减少17%。

Conclusion: 通过将安全性嵌入自动化修复的决策核心而非作为事后补救，可以在降低错误修复率的同时提升修复性能和运维效率。

Abstract: In modern IT operations (IT-Ops), the cost of an incorrect repair often exceeds the cost of no action at all. Yet existing automated remediation systems are designed to generate actions rather than to decide whether intervention is warranted, leaving safety as an afterthought enforced by manual approval. This paper makes three contributions to close this gap: (i) we reformulate safe remediation as a risk-constrained intervention decision problem and cast it as a Constrained Markov Decision Process (CMDP), in which the agent maximizes repair success subject to a bounded false remediation rate (FRR); (ii) we introduce a three-dimensional risk decomposition comprising blast radius, reversibility, and epistemic uncertainty, providing operators with an interpretable per-action safety interface; and (iii) we design a context-adaptive human-in-the-loop (HITL) gate that turns escalation from a binary failsafe into a bandwidth-aware control layer responsive to on-call load and business criticality. The full policy is learned offline from historical incident logs, enabling explicit control of the expected FRR. Experiments on the Train Ticket microservice benchmark with Chaos Mesh fault injection and an RCAEval-aligned fault taxonomy show that our framework reduces FRR by 39% while improving repair success by 2.5 points over a strong runbook baseline, and reduces on-call escalation load by 17% relative to a fixed-threshold variant.

</details>


### [90] [EvoDRC: A Self-Evolving Agentic Framework for Automated DRC Violation Repair](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20019)
*Bing-Yue Wu,Chia-Tung Ho,Haoyu Yang,Brucek Khailany,Vidya A. Chhabria*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: EvoDRC是一个基于技能演化的智能体框架，用于自动化先进节点芯片设计中的DRC修复。它通过从无关参考设计中蒸馏知识初始化层特定修复技能，并利用目标设计的可追溯修复经验持续演进这些技能。在DAC26 DRC基准测试的七个块级设计上，EvoDRC相比基线实现了73.5%的整体DRV减少。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 先进节点物理设计中，DRC闭合仍是主要瓶颈。尽管详细布线器具有规则感知能力，残留的设计规则违例(DRVs)仍需人工工程变更单迭代。自动化此过程面临挑战：修复需考虑复杂几何交互、保持电路连通性并避免引入新违例。

Method: EvoDRC框架：1) 从无关参考设计蒸馏知识初始化层特定修复技能；2) 将布局分解为有界修复区域，为每个区域分配LLM修复智能体；3) 提供局部DRC分析、连通性检查和影响预览工具反馈；4) 将修复操作及DRV变化存入知识数据库，用于演进修复技能。

Result: 在DAC26 DRC基准测试的七个块级设计上，EvoDRC相比基线实现了73.5%的整体DRV减少。

Conclusion: EvoDRC通过技能演化和知识蒸馏有效实现了自动化块级DRC修复，显著减少了设计规则违例，为先进节点物理设计中的DRC闭合问题提供了可扩展的解决方案。

Abstract: Design rule check (DRC) closure remains a major bottleneck in advanced-node physical design. Although detailed routers are rule-aware, residual design rule violations (DRVs) often require manual engineering change order iterations. Automating this process is challenging because repairs must account for complex geometric interactions, preserve circuit connectivity, and avoid introducing new violations. We present EvoDRC, a skill-evolution framework for agentic block-level DRC repair. EvoDRC initializes layer-specific repair skills using knowledge distilled from an unrelated reference design and continuously evolves these skills using traceable repair experience collected from the target design. EvoDRC decomposes the layout into bounded repair regions and assigns an LLM repair agent to each region. Local DRC analysis, connectivity-checking, and impact-preview tools provide feedback on proposed modifications. Repair operations and their resulting DRV changes are stored in a knowledge database and used to evolve the repair skills. Experiments on seven block-level designs from the DAC26 DRC Benchmark show that EvoDRC achieves a 73.5\% overall reduction compared to the reported baseline.

</details>


### [91] [Global Difference Constraint Propagation for Constraint Programming](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20022)
*Lucas Kletzander,Jip J. Dekker,Andreas Schutt,Peter J. Stuckey*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文针对差分约束 $x - y \leq d$ 的传播问题，提出构建全局（bounds consistent）传播器替代传统的单独传播器，并展示了如何在惰性子句生成求解器中为该全局传播器生成解释。实验表明，全局处理差分约束能显著优于标准传播方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 差分约束有高效的求解算法（如最短路径），但有限域传播通常将每个差分约束视为独立传播器，导致传播效率低下。本文旨在通过构建全局传播器来克服这一局限性。

Method: 借鉴SAT模理论求解器中差分约束理论求解器的思想，设计 bounds consistent 的全局差分约束传播器，并重点解决该传播器在惰性子句生成框架下的传播解释问题（explanation），使其能够集成到 lazy clause generation 求解器中。

Result: 实验结果表明，全局差分约束传播器相比标准独立传播方法在性能上有显著提升。

Conclusion: 全局处理差分约束能够大幅提高传播效率，且通过设计合适的传播解释机制，可以有效地集成到现代约束求解器中。

Abstract: Difference constraints of the form $x - y \leq d$ are well studied, with efficient algorithms for satisfaction and implication, because of their connection to shortest paths. Finite domain propagation algorithms, however, typically do not make use of these algorithms, and treat each difference constraint as a separate propagator. Propagation does guarantee completeness of solving, but can be needlessly slow. In this paper we describe how to build a (bounds consistent) global propagator for difference constraints that treats them all simultaneously. SAT modulo theory solvers have included theory solvers for difference constraints for some time. While a theory solver for difference constraints gives the basis of a global difference constraint propagator, we show how the requirements on the propagator are quite different. Crucially, we show how to explain propagations by a global difference constraint propagator, in order to use it within a lazy clause generation solver. We give experiments showing that treating difference constraints globally can substantially improve on the standard propagation approach.

</details>


### [92] [Reading and Steering Representations of Materials-Science Mechanisms in an Open-Weight Language Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20058)
*Markus J. Buehler*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文通过结合多种分析方法，证明谷歌Gemma-4-E4B-it模型中材料科学机制信息以三种可实验分离的形式存在：隐藏状态中的概念可读性、状态转换间的本构取向性、以及因果控制工程答案的内部表征。研究发现物理关系在受控状态变化中比在绝对状态中更可见。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究大型语言模型在回答科学问题时是否真正表征或使用了 governing physics，而不仅仅是产生正确输出。核心动机是揭示模型内部是否具有可分离、可解释的物理机制信息。

Method: 结合匹配的直接和Jacobian词汇读出、无选项状态几何分析、60条定律反事实基准测试以及因果干预。具体包括：三项独立拟合的Jacobian透镜复现概念排名、盲识别机制家族、状态邻域分析、图审计、方向反转提示下的隐藏状态变换分析、双向干预和反事实状态补丁。

Result: 50份材料描述中，Jacobian透镜复现了概念排名，盲识别出9/10机制家族。72提示基准显示机制特定状态邻域，但图审计表明组织可由数值比较解释。方向反转实验中，隐藏状态变换正确排序了60条关系中的39/40定向定律（词汇对照接近随机）。双向干预在所有12个匹配案例中改变了答案概率。物理关系在受控状态变化中比绝对状态更可见。

Conclusion: 大型语言模型内部确实包含可实验分离的物理机制信息，但物理关系在受控状态转换（如方向反转）中比在静态状态分析中更可检测。状态变换比绝对状态更能揭示模型的物理理解。

Abstract: Large language models can answer scientific questions, yet a correct output does not reveal whether the model represents or uses the governing physics. Here we show that materials science mechanism information in the open-weight google/gemma-4-E4B-it model has three experimentally separable forms: concepts are readable in individual hidden states, constitutive orientation is carried by controlled transformations between states, and selected internal representations causally control engineering answers. We combine matched direct and Jacobian vocabulary readouts, option-free state geometry, a 60-law counterfactual benchmark and causal interventions. In 50 held-out materials descriptions, three independently fitted Jacobian lenses reproduced concept ranks, and target-free word sets from both readouts enabled blinded identification of 9 of 10 mechanism families. A separate 72-prompt benchmark produced mechanism-specific hidden-state neighborhoods, but an exact graph audit showed that this apparent physical organization was equally explained by numerical comparison. We therefore compared otherwise identical prompts in which only the direction of the physical input was reversed, asking whether the resulting hidden-state movement followed the supplied constitutive law. These state transformations ordered direct, physically neutral and inverse laws across 60 frozen relations and correctly oriented 39 of 40 directional laws, whereas lexical controls were near chance. Bidirectional interventions shifted answer probabilities toward or away from the physically appropriate outcome across all 12 matched cases, while counterfactual state patches transferred opposing decision signals across mechanisms and answer formats. Physical relationships were therefore more visible in controlled state changes than in absolute states alone.

</details>


### [93] [PRO-LONG: Programmatic Memory Enables Long-Horizon Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20064)
*Alexis Fox,Junlin Wang,Paul Rosu,Bhuwan Dhingra*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: PRO-LONG是一个针对LLM智能体在长 horizon、探索性任务中的上下文管理框架，通过程序化记忆和结构化交互日志来缓解信息保存与检索的权衡，在ARC-AGI-3基准上平均提升18.0个百分点，同时减少4.2-5.8倍token消耗。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长 horizon 任务需要持续的感知、推理和探索，但现有LLM智能体在持续学习基准如ARC-AGI-3上表现有限。现有上下文管理方法面临关键权衡：保存更多信息导致检索相关细节的难度增加。

Method: PRO-LONG提出基于程序化记忆的极简上下文管理框架，维护完整的结构化交互日志，利用编码智能体的最新进展高效搜索该历史记录。

Result: 在完整ARC-AGI-3公共游戏集上，PRO-LONG在多个前沿模型上平均提升18.0个百分点，匹配或超越SOTA专用框架（最高76.1% pass@1），同时使用4.2-5.8倍更少的token。在Fable 5上达到97.4% best@2，总成本$1,750。

Conclusion: PRO-LONG通过程序化记忆和结构化日志有效解决了长 horizon 任务中信息保存与检索的权衡问题，在性能和效率上均优于现有方法。

Abstract: Long-horizon tasks require sustained perception, reasoning, and exploration, and are a persistent challenge for large language model (LLM) agents. This gap is reflected in their limited performance on continual learning benchmarks such as ARC-AGI-3, especially when models are evaluated out of the box. Various agent harnesses have been proposed to close this gap, and each commits to a strategy for handling long sequences of observations, i.e., what information to save from the environment and how to load it into model context, a choice we argue is particularly consequential. Existing methods for context management face a significant tradeoff, as preserving more information makes retrieving relevant details less tractable. We propose PRO-LONG, a minimal context management framework built around programmatic memory for LLM agents in long-horizon, exploratory settings. PRO-LONG addresses the tradeoff by keeping a complete, structured interaction log and capitalizing on recent progress in coding agents to search this history efficiently. On the full ARC-AGI-3 public game set, PRO-LONG improves over a base coding agent by an average of 18.0 percentage points across frontier models, and matches or exceeds state-of-the-art specialized harnesses (up to 76.1% pass@1) while using 4.2-5.8x fewer tokens. With Fable 5, PRO-LONG achieves 97.4% best@2 at a total cost of \$1,750. Relevant code and logs are available at https://github.com/alexisfox7/PRO-LONG.

</details>


### [94] [TRUST-ESD: A Risk-Calibrated and Governance-Aware AI Framework for Enterprise Strategic Decision Support Under Uncertainty](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20065)
*Tian Qiu,Li Yan,Mahabubur Rahman Miraj,Shanqin Yi,Md Intekhab Rahman Galib,Jahid Hasan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出TRUST-ESD框架，通过不确定性校准、风险评分、风险记忆、可解释性和治理验证等模块，在不确定条件下为企业战略决策提供风险校准且符合治理要求的决策支持，相比基线方法在风险调整效用、风险暴露、CVaR、校准误差、解释保真度和治理合规性等指标上均有显著提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 企业战略决策支持系统需要不仅准确，还需具备不确定性感知、风险校准、可解释性和治理合规性，而现有预测导向的方法仅通过最大化期望效用选择行动，无法平衡价值、可靠性、风险暴露和合规性等多维目标。

Method: TRUST-ESD框架包含以下核心模块：基于反事实策略的预测效用估计、共形不确定性校准、CVaR下行风险评分、风险记忆检索、策略即代码治理、可解释性分析以及人工监督机制。

Result: 相比强不确定性感知基线，TRUST-ESD在风险调整效用提升7.95%，风险暴露降低23.22%，CVaR降低23.78%，校准误差降低13.89%，解释保真度提升10.90%，治理合规性提升9.76%，同时保持具有竞争力的预测精度。

Conclusion: 消融实验和案例分析证实，不确定性校准、下行风险评分、风险记忆、可解释性和治理验证共同提升了可信赖的企业决策质量，表明该框架能有效平衡价值、可靠性、风险与合规性。

Abstract: Enterprise strategic decision support requires AI systems that are not only accurate, but also uncertainty-aware, risk-calibrated, explainable, and governance-compliant. This paper proposes TRUST-ESD, a risk-calibrated and governance-aware framework for enterprise decision support under uncertainty. TRUST-ESD evaluates feasible counterfactual strategies through predictive utility estimation, conformal uncertainty calibration, CVaR-based downside-risk scoring, risk-memory retrieval, policy-as-code governance, explainability, and human oversight. Unlike prediction-only methods that select actions by maximum expected utility, TRUST-ESD recommends strategies that balance value, reliability, risk exposure, and compliance. Experimental results show that TRUST-ESD improves risk-adjusted utility by 7.95%, reduces risk exposure by 23.22%, reduces CVaR by 23.78%, lowers calibration error by 13.89%, improves explanation fidelity by 10.90%, and increases governance compliance by 9.76% compared with strong uncertainty-aware baselines, while maintaining competitive predictive accuracy. Ablation and case-study analyses further confirm that uncertainty calibration, downside-risk scoring, risk memory, explainability, and governance validation jointly improve trustworthy enterprise decision-making.

</details>


### [95] [CUSUM-Shaped Inference-Time Monitoring and Targeted Re-Decoding for Quantized Small Language Model Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20129)
*El Hassane Ettifouri,Ayoub Belfatmi,Mahaman Sanoussi Yahaya Alassan,Walid Dahhane*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出MGT-B（监测引导的测试时回溯）方法，通过在自回归推理模型的解码过程中监测不确定性并检测退化特征，在触发警报时回溯到较早状态并重新解码，从而避免模型陷入冗长、重复或无效的推理轨迹。在MATH-500数据集上的评估显示，该方法在240对时间序列验证集上准确率从82/240提升至88/240（+2.50个百分点，McNemar p=0.2632），在467对历史覆盖集上从146/467提升至167/467（+4.50个百分点，McNemar p=0.000753），但两项分析均非确认性，且经验因子尚未被确立为有效的e-过程或e-检测器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量化的小型自回归推理模型可能进入冗长、重复或无效的推理轨迹，而现有的推理时计算分配通常不观察轨迹的发展情况，缺乏对推理过程的监测和干预机制。

Method: 基于早期token级e-CUSUM控制器，MGT-B将采样前的不确定性和退化特征的重叠窗口映射到位置条件经验尾概率，使用CUSUM形状重置机制累积混合投注因子，在触发警报时估计回滚点，恢复token和KV缓存状态，并执行约束性重新解码。

Result: 在240对时间序列验证集上准确率从82/240提升至88/240（+2.50个百分点；13次纠正，7次回归；精确McNemar p=0.2632；配对bootstrap 95%区间[-1.25, +6.25]）。在467对历史覆盖集上准确率从146/467提升至167/467（+4.50个百分点；McNemar p=0.000753）。316个无警报输出与原始结果完全一致，151个触发警报的轨迹中包含29次纠正和8次回归。

Conclusion: 结果支持在所研究的MATH-500设置中存在选择性监测与修复机制，而非通用或理论上可证明的推理改进。该方法尚不能视为确认性发现，经验因子也未确立为有效的e-过程或e-检测器。

Abstract: Quantized small autoregressive reasoning models can enter long, repetitive, or unproductive trajectories, yet inference-time compute is usually allocated without observing how a trajectory develops. Building on an earlier token-level e-CUSUM controller, we develop MGT-B (Monitoring-Guided Test-time Backtracking), a revised external controller that maps overlapping windows of pre-sampling uncertainty and degeneration features to position-conditional empirical tail probabilities, accumulates mixture betting factors with a CUSUM-shaped reset, and responds to an alarm by estimating a rollback point, restoring token and key-value-cache state, and performing constrained re-decoding. To audit whether the effect persists on problem identities first observed after the manual choice of log threshold h = 10, we retrospectively exclude 260 IDs present in pre-threshold artifacts and retain the chronologically first post-threshold pair for each remaining ID, yielding a 240-pair chronology-audit set. On this set, accuracy changes from 82/240 to 88/240 (+2.50 percentage points; 13 corrections, 7 regressions; exact McNemar p = 0.2632; paired bootstrap 95% interval [-1.25, +6.25]). A broader 467-pair historical-coverage set of seed-matched pairs changes accuracy from 146/467 to 167/467 (+4.50 points; McNemar p = 0.000753), but includes 200 seed-1 IDs available before or during threshold selection and is reported only as an exploratory estimate. All 316 no-alarm outputs in the 467-pair set are identical to vanilla, while the 151 alarmed trajectories contain 29 corrections and 8 regressions. Neither analysis is confirmatory, and the empirical factors are not established as a valid e-process or e-detector. The results support a selective monitoring-and-repair mechanism for the studied MATH-500 setting, rather than a general or theoretically certified reasoning improvement.

</details>


### [96] [PoTRE: Test-Time Reasoning inspired by Cognitive Heterogeneity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20268)
*Anmol Kankariya,Sercan Ö. Arık*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: PoTRE是一种多智能体异构推理框架，通过四个解耦的推理智能体（对抗精炼、分层规划、频谱搜索、直接链）和任务自适应聚合层，在ARC-AGI-2、HLE和PRBench Finance基准上取得SOTA性能，在HLE上达到49.92%准确率，且推理效率优于大规模同质基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM在需要长程规划和迭代纠错的复杂推理任务中表现不佳，且单流提示在应对新抽象或严格领域约束时鲁棒性差。

Method: 提出PoTRE异构推理框架，将推理解耦为四个智能体：对抗精炼、分层策略规划、频谱搜索和直接链推理；通过任务自适应聚合层（候选选择、语义合成或神经符号验证）动态整合多视角输出。

Result: 在HLE上达到49.92%准确率，超越此前官方最佳成绩；在ARC-AGI-2和PRBench Finance上也取得SOTA；使用相似或更少的推理token，性能优于大规模同质化基线。

Conclusion: 架构异质性能够以更少的计算资源实现更优的复杂推理性能，为LLM推理提供了高效且鲁棒的新范式。

Abstract: While Large Language Models (LLMs) excel at many tasks, they frequently struggle with complex reasoning that requires long-horizon planning and iterative error correction. Furthermore, standard single-stream prompting proves brittle when models encounter novel abstractions or rigorous domain constraints. We introduce PoTRE (Poly-Topological Reasoning Ensembles), a heterogeneous framework that decouples inference into four agents: (1) Adversarial Refinement Agent, (2) Hierarchical strategic Planning Agent, (3) Spectrum Search Agent, and (4) Direct Chain Agent. A final Task-Adaptive Aggregation Layer dynamically reconciles these perspectives -- via final candidate selection, semantic synthesis, or neuro-symbolic verification -- to produce a robust global solution. We evaluate PoTRE on three frontier benchmarks: ARC-AGI-2, Humanity's Last Exam (HLE), and PRBench Finance. PoTRE achieves state-of-the-art accuracy of 49.92% on HLE, surpassing the previous best official score. We demonstrate that this architectural heterogeneity achieves improved reasoning performance using similar or fewer inference tokens compared to heavily scaled homogeneous baselines.

</details>


### [97] [Train the Model, Not the Reader: Decodability Supervision for Verifiable Activation Explanations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20379)
*Hiskias Dingeto*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文揭示自然语言自编码器对隐藏层激活的解释存在严重缺陷：高重构分数并不保证解释中的具体声明为真。作者提出RECAP方法，通过共训练线性头使模型内部指定内容可被独立解码，从而提供可验证的 faithfulness 保障。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自然语言自编码器以重构分数衡量解释的 faithfulness，但该测试对个别虚假声明结构不敏感——翻转某个声明若不影响重构，该声明就不会受到惩罚。作者试图揭示这一缺陷并寻找解决方案。

Method: (1) 通过实验证明重构测试可被两种不忠实的方式通过：解释重构远高于随机水平，但仅约2%的具体声明是重构依赖的，说明分数追踪的是大意而非具体事实；在精确合成真实条件下，标准方法会在5/5运行中产生共适应的私密编码。 (2) 提出两个审计协议：grounded-vs-true cross 和 evaluator swap。 (3) 提出RECAP方法：在与目标模型共训练的同时训练线性头，使指定内容保持可解码状态。

Result: RECAP训练的沙盒模型上，新verbalizer能真正陈述指定内容，私密编码消失，代价仅为+0.001 nat。在预训练的Pythia-160M上，指定内容变得可被探针可靠解码，但新verbalizer仅能部分传达（truth 0.44-0.46 vs 近零控制）。RECAP使独立探针能区分verbalizer的真假声明（AUC 0.96 vs 无RECAP的0.82）。面对对抗攻击时，RECAP探针仍能检测谎言（AUC 0.95），控制探针则降至随机水平（0.51）。

Conclusion: 高重构分数不能认证个别声明的真实性。RECAP使指定内部内容可独立于模型生成的散文进行可验证检测，为可解释性和AI安全提供了更可靠的工具。

Abstract: Natural-language autoencoders score explanations of hidden activations by reconstruction: an explanation is deemed faithful if the activation can be regenerated from it. The test is structurally insensitive to individual false claims: if flipping a claim does not change the reconstruction, the claim is never penalized. We show the test is passed in two ways, neither faithful. On a released Qwen-2.5-7B verbalizer, explanations reconstruct well above chance while ~2% of specific claims are reconstruction-dependent, so the score tracks gist, not specific facts. Under exact synthetic ground truth, the standard recipe develops co-adapted private codes (false wording the reconstruction depends on) in 5/5 runs, and fixes that leave the target model unchanged do not help. We contribute two audit protocols, the grounded-vs-true cross and the evaluator swap, and RECAP (Readable Encodings via Co-trained Auxiliary Predictors): linear heads trained alongside the target model to keep designated content decodable. On RECAP-trained sandbox models, fresh verbalizers state the designated content truly and the codes vanish, at a +0.001-nat cost. This replicates on a pretrained Pythia-160M: the content becomes reliably probe-decodable, though a fresh verbalizer conveys it only in part (truth 0.44-0.46 vs a near-zero control). For interpretability, high reconstruction does not certify individual claims. For AI safety, RECAP makes designated internal content independently checkable against probes rather than asserted by prose a model can game: an independent probe scores the verbalizer's true claims above its false ones (AUC 0.96, vs 0.82 without RECAP). Against an adversary that edits an explanation to maximize the reconstruction score while lying (suppressing ~87% of its lie penalty), the RECAP probe still flags the lies (AUC 0.95) while the control probe collapses to chance (0.51).

</details>


### [98] [SoftReason: A Fully Differentiable Neuro-Soft-Symbolic Deductive Reasoning Architecture over High-Dimensional Perceptual Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20402)
*Wael AbdAlmageed*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: SoftReason是一种神经软符号架构，通过可微分的软解释张量实现感知与演绎推理的端到端可微分离散接口，在KVQA任务中支持感知接地、知识图谱证据注入和可微分演绎闭包。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统神经符号系统在感知和演绎之间存在离散接口，导致梯度无法反向传播；同时推理问题中前提需从高维输入推断而非离散符号，且谓词词汇、论元结构和可信证据由知识图谱或规则定义提供，现有方法难以实现端到端可微分训练。

Method: 核心创新是学习可微分的直接后果算子（immediate-consequence operator）的提升版本：使用谓词定义嵌入和潜在组合通道形成软体谓词混合，聚合所有可能见证者，提出查询条件化的头部事实，通过单调概率OR更新软解释张量。感知模块输出概率化基础事实，知识图谱三元组以高置信度软证据形式注入。

Result: 在Knowledge-aware Visual Question Answering（KVQA）任务上实例化，证明了SoftReason在单一可训练架构中支持端到端感知接地、知识图谱证据注入和可微分演绎闭包的有效性。

Conclusion: SoftReason成功弥合了感知与演绎之间的梯度鸿沟，提供了在连续推理中同时利用感知数据和结构化知识进行可微分推理的通用框架。

Abstract: In many reasoning problems, the premises are not observed as discrete symbols, but must be inferred from high-dimensional inputs. Further, the predicate vocabulary, argument structure, and trusted evidence are supplied by a Knowledge Graph (KG), or rule definitions. Classical neuro-symbolic pipelines have a discrete interface between perception and deduction. We present a neuro-soft-symbolic architecture for differentiable deductive reasoning over latent perceptual facts and knowledge-provided predicates. SoftReason removes the gradient gap by representing the deductive state as a local soft interpretation tensor over candidate constants and predicates. Perception proposes probabilistic base facts, KG triples enter as high-confidence soft evidence, and every query anchor, predicate choice, and closure update remains differentiable. Our core innovation is a learned differentiable lift of the immediate-consequence operator. It uses predicate-definition embeddings and latent composition channels to form soft body-predicate mixtures, aggregate over all possible witnesses, propose query-conditioned head facts, and update the interpretation through a monotone probabilistic OR. We instantiate the framework on Knowledge-aware Visual Question Answering (KVQA), and demonstrates how SoftReason supports end-to-end perceptual grounding, KG evidence injection, and differentiable deductive closure in one trainable architecture.

</details>


<div id='cs.CR'></div>

# cs.CR [[Back]](#toc)

### [99] [JailMeter: An Evidence-Based Evaluation Framework for Jailbreak Attacks on Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19424)
*Qingjia Huang,Jingyu Zhang,Jianguo Wu,Yakai Li,Weijuan Zhang,Yankai Rong,Junyi Yao,Shengzhi Zhang,Xiaoqi Jia*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 提出了JailMeter，一个基于信息瓶颈理论的双重反馈优化框架，用于更准确地评估大语言模型的越狱攻击效果，在包含330个人工标注样本的基准上达到97.27%的准确率，并蒸馏出轻量级版本JailMeter_SLM以降低计算成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前对大型语言模型越狱攻击的评估存在标准和方法不一致的问题，导致攻击成功率的估计不可靠，亟需更忠实的评估框架。

Method: 基于信息瓶颈理论，应用双重反馈优化（dual-feedback optimization）从模型响应中过滤越狱噪声，同时保留与原始恶意问题相关的内容，生成简洁证据进行严格评估。仅当响应捕获恶意意图并提供完整答案时才判定攻击成功。

Result: 在包含330个人工标注的非拒绝越狱实例的JailMeter-Eva基准上，JailMeter准确率达到97.27%，显著优于现有评估方法。蒸馏后的JailMeter_SLM在保持可比可靠性的同时大幅降低计算成本。

Conclusion: JailMeter为越狱攻击评估提供了更可靠、更高效的解决方案，有助于推动大语言模型安全对齐研究的发展。

Abstract: The assessment of jailbreak attacks against large language models currently suffers from inconsistent evaluation criteria and methods, leading to unreliable estimates of attack success rates. We propose JailMeter, an evidence-based evaluation framework designed to more faithfully measure jailbreak effectiveness. Inspired by the Information Bottleneck theory, JailMeter applies dual-feedback optimization to filter jailbreak noise from model responses while preserving content relevant to the original malicious question. This process produces concise evidence for a rigorous assessment under which an attack is validated only when the response captures the malicious intent and delivers a complete answer, thereby signaling a substantive bypass of model safety alignment. We evaluate JailMeter on JailMeter-Eva, a challenging benchmark containing 330 human-labeled, non-rejected jailbreak instances. JailMeter achieves an accuracy of 97.27%, substantially outperforming existing evaluation methods. To support large-scale evaluation, we further distill JailMeter into a small language model, JailMeter\textsubscript{SLM}, which maintains comparable reliability with significantly reduced computational costs. Code and dataset are available at https://github.com/Magi2B0y/JailMeter.

</details>


### [100] [ChannelGuard: Safe Models Do Not Compose into Safe Multi-Agent Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19430)
*Elias Hossain,Md Mehedi Hasan Nipu,Fatema Tuj Johora Faria,Tasfia Nuzhat Ornee,Maleeha Sheikh*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文揭示了多智能体LLM应用中，智能体间未监控的通道存在安全漏洞，现有防御仅保护输入边界或依赖外部过滤器。作者提出ChannelGuard，一种无需训练的深度防御框架，在智能体间通道设置信息瓶颈门控，通过嵌入相似性对文本进行放行、压缩或阻断，无需额外LLM调用。实验表明，ChannelGuard在工具投毒攻击上实现100%阻断（30/30），且在不同后端上表现一致，而未被保护的管道则完全依赖于云提供商过滤器。提示注入攻击成功减半，GSM8K准确率保持0.867不变。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究多关注LLM应用的输入边界防御（如IBProtector、Llama Guard等）或外部过滤器，但忽略了多智能体LLM管道中智能体间每个跳跃点都是未监控通道，攻击者可通过这些通道注入恶意指令。作者通过实验证明，一个看似安全的管道其安全性几乎完全依赖云提供商的服务器端过滤器，而非应用自身的防御能力，且在不同后端上表现差异巨大。这种依赖关系被传统的结果报告所掩盖。

Method: ChannelGuard是一种无需训练（training-free）的深度防御框架。核心设计是在每个智能体间通道上设置信息瓶颈门控（information-bottleneck gates），每个门控通过嵌入相似性（embedding similarity）对通道文本进行评分，与对抗性短语库（adversarial phrase bank）匹配，然后确定性执行三类操作之一：放行（pass）、压缩（compress）或阻断（block）。整个过程中不添加任何LLM调用。同时，框架内置归因方法（attribution method），记录每一层由哪个门控阻止了攻击。

Result: 在8个攻击家族、5种防御、3种模型后端、2100条轨迹的评估中：（1）ChannelGuard的工具输出门控在Azure GPT-5、Anthropic Sonnet 4.5和Anthropic Haiku 4.5上均100%阻断工具投毒（30/30），而未经保护的管道在后端间表现完全不一致；（2）提示注入攻击成功率从0.333降至0.167；（3）GSM8K准确率保持0.867不变；（4）白盒自适应释义（adaptive paraphrase）可绕过所有嵌入门控，但扰动-投票（perturb-and-vote）基线表现更佳。附录包含消融实验、良性保留分析、评估者一致性检验（kappa=0.900），总成本仅47.36美元。

Conclusion: 多智能体LLM应用的安全评估不能仅依赖结果报告（outcome-only reporting），因为这种报告会隐藏对云提供商过滤器的依赖。ChannelGuard提供了一种轻量级、跨后端一致的深度防御方案，在不影响主要任务性能的前提下显著增强安全性，但面对自适应白盒攻击时仍需改进。

Abstract: Multi-agent LLM applications chain a planner, worker agents, a verifier, and a synthesizer, and every hop between agents is an unmonitored channel through which an adversary can smuggle instructions. Existing defenses guard only the input boundary (IBProtector, Llama Guard, perplexity filters, SmoothLLM) or run outside the application as opaque, stochastic provider-side filters. We show this gap carries a consequence rarely measured: on a 2,100-trace evaluation across eight attack families, five defenses, and three model backends, an undefended pipeline that appears fully safe under standard reporting (attack success 0.000 on tool- and memory-poisoning) owes that safety almost entirely to the cloud provider's server-side filter (54 of 60 blocks on Azure GPT-5), and silently shifts to the agent model's own alignment on a backend without such a filter. Outcome-only reporting hides this dependence. We present ChannelGuard, a training-free defense-in-depth framework placing information-bottleneck gates on every inter-agent channel; each scores channel text against an adversarial phrase bank by embedding similarity and deterministically passes, compresses, or blocks it, adding no LLM call, while an attribution method records which layer stopped each attack. ChannelGuard's tool-output gate blocks Tool Poisoning 30 of 30 at the application layer, identically across Azure GPT-5, Anthropic Sonnet 4.5, and Anthropic Haiku 4.5, whereas the undefended pipeline shifts entirely across backends; it also lowers Prompt Injection attack success by half (0.333 to 0.167) and preserves GSM8K accuracy exactly (0.867). White-box adaptive paraphrase evades every embedding gate, where a perturb-and-vote baseline does better. An extended appendix adds baselines, ablations, sweeps, a benign-preservation analysis, and a judge audit (kappa = 0.900), at a total cost of 47.36 USD.

</details>


### [101] [ChainWatch: A Kill Chain-Aligned Sequential Detection Framework for Multi-Step Attacks in MCP-Based AI Agent Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19432)
*Om Narayan,Rashmi Jyoti,Ramkinker Singh*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: ChainWatch是一个基于隐马尔可夫模型（HMM）的序列检测框架，用于识别MCP（模型上下文协议）AI代理系统中由多个工具调用组合而成的多步攻击链，能够检测到传统逐调用防护机制无法发现的恶意序列。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MCP协议允许AI代理连接外部工具和服务，带来了强大的能力，但也引入了多步攻击的风险。攻击者可以将单个无害的工具调用组合成恶意序列，现有的逐调用防护机制无法可靠检测这类攻击，因此需要能够识别工具调用序列中恶意模式的检测框架。

Method: ChainWatch采用六阶段杀伤链（kill chain）对攻击进程建模，使用隐马尔可夫模型（HMM）对工具调用序列进行分类。当会话在多个阶段中表现出可疑进展时触发检测规则。框架包含一个结构化的威胁模型，涵盖直接序列攻击、间接提示注入链和混合多阶段攻击，并设计了20维特征提取模式来捕获工具交互中的行为信号。

Result: 使用安全文献中五个代表性攻击场景进行演示，结果表明ChainWatch能够检测到那些传统逐调用安全机制无法发现的攻击链。

Conclusion: ChainWatch通过序列化检测方法有效弥补了传统逐调用防护在MCP多步攻击检测上的不足，为MCP-based AI代理系统提供了更全面的安全保障。

Abstract: The Model Context Protocol (MCP) is an open-source standard that allows AI agents to connect to external tools, databases, and services. While this connectivity enables powerful agent capabilities, it also introduces multi-step attacks that existing per-call defenses cannot reliably detect. Attackers can compose individually benign tool invocations into malicious sequences that evade isolated inspection. This paper presents ChainWatch, a sequential detection framework for identifying multi-step attacks in MCP-based AI agent systems. ChainWatch models attack progression using a six-stage kill chain and applies a Hidden Markov Model (HMM) to classify tool-call sequences. Detection rules are triggered when a session exhibits suspicious progression across multiple stages. The framework is supported by a structured threat model covering direct sequential attacks, indirect prompt injection chains, and hybrid multi-stage attacks. A 20-dimensional feature extraction schema captures behavioral signals from tool interactions. We demonstrate the approach using five representative attack scenarios from the security literature, showing how ChainWatch detects attack chains that evade traditional per-call security mechanisms.

</details>


### [102] [Building Trust in Autonomous Commerce: A Verifiable Global Event Timeline and AI-Ready Fraud Intelligence Layer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19436)
*Rajat Srivastava*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文针对代理商务协议缺乏跨域可验证审计和时序证明的问题，提出基于规范事件模式、确定性批次排序、Merkle追加式承诺和区块链锚定的可验证全局事件时间线，并引入加密签名的欺诈标记和数据集血统模型。实验表明：Merkle树构建5万事件仅需47毫秒，端到端验证低于0.013毫秒，包含证明从320字节（1千事件）对数增长至512字节（5万事件），验证性能比线性扫描提升14.4倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有代理商务协议（如AP2、ACP）支持安全代理发起交易，但缺乏跨异构域的可互操作、防篡改审计能力，以及可验证的事件时序排序机制。

Method: 提出由四个核心组件构成的全局事件时间线：1）规范事件模式（保证确定性序列化）；2）确定性批次形成（不依赖同步时钟的可复现排序）；3）基于Merkle的仅追加承诺（对数级包含证明）；4）区块链锚定（防篡改时间骨架）。在此基础上引入加密签名的欺诈标记和数据集血统模型。

Result: 原型实验数据显示：Merkle树构建5万事件耗时47毫秒；端到端验证无论批次大小均在0.013毫秒内完成；包含证明从1千事件的320字节对数增长到5万事件的512字节；Merkle验证在5万事件时比线性扫描快14.4倍。

Conclusion: 提出的可验证全局事件时间线和相关组件有效填补了代理商务协议在可互操作审计和时序验证方面的空白，为代理驱动的AI交易提供了防篡改、可验证的基础设施。

Abstract: Agentic commerce protocols such as AP2 and ACP define mechanisms for secure agent-initiated transactions but do not provide interoperable, tamper-evident auditability or verifiable temporal ordering of events across heterogeneous domains. This paper addresses these gaps by proposing a verifiable global event timeline for agentic commerce, constructed from four core components: canonical event schemas that enforce deterministic serialization, deterministic batch formation ensuring reproducible ordering without reliance on synchronized clocks, Merkle-based append-only commitments providing logarithmic-cost inclusion proofs, and blockchain anchoring establishing a tamper-evident temporal backbone. Building on this infrastructure, we introduce a cryptographically signed fraud marker that binds risk labels to anchored evidence through an unforgeable provenance chain, and a dataset lineage model enabling reproducible, tamper-evident AI training pipelines. Empirical results from a prototype implementation demonstrate: Merkle tree construction processes 50,000 events in 47 milliseconds; end-to-end verification completes in under 0.013 milliseconds regardless of batch size; inclusion proof sizes grow logarithmically from 320 bytes at 1,000 events to 512 bytes at 50,000 events; and Merkle-based verification outperforms linear scan by 14.4x at 50,000 events.

</details>


### [103] [Intelligent Disruption: Undetectable Attacks on Wireless Autoencoders](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19448)
*Han Jiang,Jifa Zhang,Hu Jin,Nan Zhao,Mingqian Liu,Yunfei Chen*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 针对多智能体对抗攻击场景中累积泄漏干扰降低攻击隐蔽性、动态环境降低攻击稳定性的问题，提出了一种基于深度学习的智能攻击框架，通过深度神经网络功率控制减少干扰泄漏，并利用条件生成对抗网络生成自适应扰动信号，在隐蔽性、攻击性和适应性上均优于基准方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在多对抗者复杂场景下，多个并行攻击产生的累积泄漏干扰(CLI)会提高攻击被检测的概率，同时动态环境使得固定攻击策略难以保持稳定的有效性，亟需一种能联合提升攻击隐蔽性、攻击性和适应性的方法。

Method: 提出深度学习智能攻击框架：(1) 基于深度神经网络的发射功率控制，通过调节多个对抗者的发射功率来减少干扰泄漏，提升隐蔽性；(2) 条件生成对抗攻击，以攻击信道信息为条件输入，生成器产生扰动信号使受攻击接收信号接近干净信号以迷惑判别器，通过对抗训练学习自适应扰动。

Result: 仿真结果表明，所提框架在攻击隐蔽性、攻击性和适应性方面均优于基准方法。

Conclusion: 所提出的深度学习智能攻击框架有效解决了多对抗者场景下的隐蔽性、攻击性和适应性问题，为无线自编码器通信中的对抗攻击提供了新方案。

Abstract: Adversarial attacks can degrade the legitimate decision performance in wireless autoencoder communications. However, in complex scenarios with multiple adversaries, the cumulative leakage interference (CLI) caused by the multiple parallel attacks increases the chance of detecting the attacks, while dynamical environments also make the fixed attack strategies difficult to have stable effectiveness. To jointly enhance the undetectability, aggressivity and adaptability of adversarial attacks, we propose a deep learning based intelligent attack framework. Specifically, considering the CLI caused by the multiple parallel attacks, a deep neural network based transmit power control is established to reduce the interference leakage by regulating the transmit power of these adversaries, thereby improving the undetectability. Furthermore, to enhance the attack effectiveness and stability in the dynamic environment, the conditional generative adversarial attack is further developed. The generator takes the attack channel information as the conditional input to produce the perturbating signals to mislead the discriminator by making the attacked received signals resemble the clean received signals, while the discriminator distinguishes between the two under the same condition. Through the adversarial training, the generator can learn to create adaptive perturbating signals with enhanced attack performance. Simulation results demonstrate that the proposed framework outperforms benchmarks in terms of attack undetectability, aggressivity and adaptability.

</details>


### [104] [Integrity of peer-to-peer distributed LLM inference under malicious nodes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19490)
*Mert Cihangiroglu,Antonino Nocera*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 提出一种基于金丝雀输入（canary inputs）的分布式LLM推理完整性校验方法，通过测量激活值漂移分布来检测恶意节点，无需固定阈值，在408个配置中达到AUROC 1.0。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在P2P分布式LLM推理中，多个独立方控制的节点可能篡改输出，现有完整性检查方法（如已知答案陷阱、密码学承诺）只检验精确正确性，无法容忍良性节点间由硬件噪声引起的正常波动。

Method: 选取少量秘密金丝雀输入，预先计算其正确激活值，混入常规查询流量中；由于节点无法区分金丝雀与真实查询，篡改节点也会破坏金丝雀；通过比较实际激活值与参考值的偏差来检测恶意行为，将恶意节点识别建模为两个漂移分布的概率检验，不使用固定阈值。

Result: 在408种配置下（指标和成功标准在实验前固定），检测器达到AUROC 1.0，即在每个配置的每个金丝雀上，恶意分片的排名始终高于所有良性分片。

Conclusion: 该方法能有效检测分布式LLM推理中的节点篡改行为，同时容忍良性节点间的正常硬件噪声波动，为P2P分布式推理提供实用的完整性保障。

Abstract: Peer-to-peer distributed inference executes a Large Language Model (LLM) on pooled consumer hardware by spreading its layers across many nodes. Every request passes through nodes that are owned and controlled by multiple independent parties. However, in this setting, any party can tamper with the output of its layers to corrupt the end result. Recomputing the forward pass on trusted hardware can catch this, but it introduces additional computational cost. The scientific literature includes several prior integrity-checking approaches, such as known-answer traps for image classifiers and cryptographic commitments. However, these solutions test only the exact correctness and do not account for the ordinary variation that may arise between benign nodes. In this paper, we propose a method that checks the output integrity by measuring the variation in the activations that each node passes to the next. A peer who wants to use the network selects a small set of secret canary inputs whose correct activations are known in advance and mixes them into regular traffic. Because the peers cannot tell a canary from a real query, any tampering node corrupts them as well. The deviation from the known reference then reveals malicious activity: benign nodes exhibit only minor variation from hardware-induced noise, whereas tampered nodes deviate far more. We treat the identification of malicious nodes as a probabilistic test that separates two drift distributions, without relying on a fixed threshold. We study 408 configurations with metrics and success criteria fixed before any experiment ran; the detector reaches AUROC 1.0, correctly ranking the malicious shard above every benign shard on every canary in every configuration.

</details>


### [105] [When HTTP 402 Meets the Blockchain: Risks on Emerging x402 Payments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19545)
*Qinying Wang,Yong Yang,Yuan Chen,Shouling Ji,Mathias Payer*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文首次系统研究了基于x402支付协议（扩展HTTP 402）的第三方中介安全现状，定义了8条安全规则，提出了4类新攻击向量（免费购物、资产窃取、服务拒绝、Gas滥用），并对15个主流中介（服务超6万卖家和36万买家）进行自动化黑盒测试，发现所有中介均存在违规问题，其中Coinbase等已采纳修复建议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: x402协议正被大型厂商快速采用并有主网经济活动，但实际部署的安全性尚未被系统研究。由于中介集中化了信任和验证，单一漏洞可能波及大量服务，亟需对其安全态势进行全面评估。

Method: (1) 定义8条面向中介的关键安全规则；(2) 基于规则违规分析，推导出4种新攻击向量；(3) 提出半自动化黑盒测试工具，对15个主流x402中介进行大规模评估；(4) 对超过1.19亿笔Base和Solana链上交易进行实证测量，量化协议采纳度、中介集中度和生态风险指标。

Result: 所有15个被评估的中介均存在安全规则违规；发现4类攻击可导致商户直接经济损失、中介资产被盗、赞助方Gas/费用无限消耗及支付服务中断；链上数据揭示了显著的x402采纳量和中介集中化风险。

Conclusion: 当前x402中介实现普遍存在安全缺陷，亟需加强安全规范；负责任披露已促使受影响方（包括Coinbase）采纳缓解措施；研究方法和工具为协议安全评估提供了基础。

Abstract: x402 is an emerging payment protocol for Web APIs and autonomous AI agents. x402 extends HTTP 402 with a payment negotiation flow and delegates payment proof verification and on-chain settlement to third-party facilitators. As a result, facilitators serve as a shared payment infrastructure for many independent merchants. This centralizes trust and validation in one component, so a single flaw can affect many services. Despite rapid adoption by major vendors and economically meaningful mainnet activity, the security posture of real-world x402 deployments remains poorly characterized.
  We present the first systematic study of authorization correctness and execution safety in current facilitator-mediated x402 deployments in the wild, identifying eight security rules for facilitators as critical payment infrastructure. Based on our analysis of rule violations, we derive four new attack vectors, including Free Shopping, Asset Theft, Service Denial, and Gas Abuse. These attacks exploit weaknesses in the real-world facilitator and server implementations and cause severe harm, including direct financial loss to merchants, theft of facilitator-held assets, unbounded sponsor-paid gas/fees, and disruption of payment services. To assess the security of x402 deployments at scale, we propose a semi-automated black-box tool and apply it to 15 major x402 facilitators collectively used by over 60K sellers and 360K buyers. Alarmingly, we find violations in all evaluated facilitators. We responsibly disclosed our findings to the affected parties, who acknowledged the issues and adopted mitigations, including changes by Coinbase. Finally, we complement our controlled testing with an empirical measurement of over 119 million recent Base and Solana transactions, quantifying x402 adoption, facilitator centralization, and ecosystem-level risk indicators.

</details>


### [106] [Examining User Behavior and Cognitive Biases in Personal Password Security](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19586)
*Evelyn Crowe,Patralika Ghosh,Shreyas Kumar,Rebecca Schlegel,Rebecca Ward,Guofei Gu*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 用户虽然意识到网络安全风险，但受即时便利偏好和心理偏见影响，仍持续采取不安全的密码实践；研究通过调查揭示行为经济学因素（双曲贴现、现状偏见、当下偏见）导致安全拖延和抵抗更安全认证方式，建议设计符合真实决策倾向的用户友好型认证政策。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究用户在网络安全意识提高的情况下仍坚持不安全密码实践（如密码复用、选择弱凭证、忽视安全建议）的原因，理解行为与认知因素对密码决策的影响。

Method: 采用问卷调查方法，分析用户创建、存储和管理密码的方式，结合行为经济学理论（双曲贴现、现状偏见、当下偏见）解释安全习惯是否随时间改善。

Result: 即时便利性往往优先于长期安全考量，用户更注重密码可记忆性而非强度；关键心理偏见导致安全拖延和抗拒采用更安全的认证方式（如密码管理器和多因素认证）。

Conclusion: 弥合安全认知与实际行动之间的差距，为设计符合真实决策倾向的用户友好型认证策略提供实用见解，推动以人为中心的安全研究方向。

Abstract: Despite increasing awareness of cybersecurity risks, users continue to engage in insecure password practices, such as reusing passwords, choosing weak credentials, and neglecting security recommendations. The study explores the behavioral and cognitive factors that influence password decision-making by integrating insights from behavioral economics, particularly hyperbolic discounting, status quo bias, and present bias. We conducted a survey to analyze how people create, store and manage their passwords, examining whether security habits have improved over time in response to greater awareness. Our findings reveal that immediate convenience often outweighs long-term security considerations, leading users to prioritize memorability over strength. Additionally, we identify key psychological biases that contribute to security procrastination and resistance to adopting more secure authentication practices, such as password managers and multi-factor authentication. The study contributes to human-centered security by bridging the gap between security awareness and action, offering practical insights to design user-friendly authentication policies that align with real-world decision-making tendencies.

</details>


### [107] [Twin Agent: Context Residual Compression for Privilege Separated Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19595)
*Zhanhao Hu,Dennis Jacob,Xiao Huang,Zhaorun Chen,Bo Li,David Wagner*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 提出Twin Agent设计模式，通过探索代理和安全代理的对称分离，在保持任务效用的同时防御提示注入攻击，在SWE-bench Lite、AgentDojo和DecodingTrust-Agent上优于现有基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM代理易受提示注入攻击，而安全设计方法（如权限分离）虽能缓解风险但常降低任务效用且需要大量工程适配，缺乏通用且高效的解决方案。

Method: 受残差编码启发，设计两个近乎对称的代理：Explore Agent检查不可信信息，Safe Agent执行特权操作；Explore Agent基于Safe Agent当前上下文，仅向Safe Agent传递关于下一步行动的紧凑线索，从而在安全性与效用性之间取得更好权衡。

Result: 在SWE-bench Lite（长程软件工程任务）、AgentDojo和DecodingTrust-Agent（异构多工具交互任务）上，Twin Agent在保持高任务效用的同时有效防御提示注入攻击，性能优于未防御代理和权限分离基线。

Conclusion: 通过紧凑线索传递的对称代理分离设计能实现更好的安全-效用权衡，是一种通用且有效的提示注入防御模式。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) agents are vulnerable to security risks, such as prompt injection attacks from untrusted context that manipulate downstream reasoning and tool use. Existing secure-by-design approaches mitigate this risk by separating untrusted observations from privileged execution and careful control of information flow, but often degrade utility and require extensive task-specific engineering. We thus propose Twin Agent, a general privilege separation design pattern inspired by residual coding in the agent context. Twin Agent consists of two nearly symmetric agents: an Explore Agent that inspects untrusted information and a Safe Agent that executes privileged actions. The Explore Agent is conditioned on the Safe Agent's current context and communicates only compact hints to the Safe Agent about the next action to take. This design reduces the information needed to preserve task utility and thus achieves a better security--utility tradeoff, which we empirically verify by measuring how utility and attack success change as the length of hints varies. We evaluate Twin Agent on long-horizon software engineering tasks with SWE-bench Lite and on heterogeneous multi-tool interaction tasks with AgentDojo and DecodingTrust-Agent. Across both benchmarks, Twin Agent preserves high task utility while preventing prompt injection attacks, outperforming both undefended agents and privilege separation baselines.

</details>


### [108] [FedLSG: LLM-Enhanced Semantic Calibration for Federated Graph Backdoor Defense](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19674)
*Chenyu Zhou,Yabin Peng,Wei Huang,Kunlin Li,Shuaishuai Zhang,Xinyuan Miao*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: FedLSG是首个将大语言模型(LLM)集成到联邦图神经网络后门防御中的框架，通过图和行为到文本的语义转换以及师生架构，有效抵抗后门攻击而不损害图结构完整性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有FedGNN后门防御依赖基于规则的方案，缺乏语义理解能力，难以抵御隐蔽触发器的攻击，且容易破坏良性图结构。

Method: 提出图和行为到文本的语义转换机制，将局部图结构和客户端更新行为转化为自然语言表示；采用轻量级师生架构：服务端使用全规模LLM作为教师进行全局语义指导和恶意参与者检测，客户端维护LoRA学生模型进行语义推理以抑制后门触发器相关边的影响。

Result: 实验表明FedLSG显著提升了对后门攻击的抵抗能力，同时保持了图结构的完整性。

Conclusion: 通过将语义理解引入联邦图后门防御，实现了对图模式和客户端行为的可解释性防御，克服了传统规则方法的局限。

Abstract: Federated Graph Neural Networks (FedGNNs) are highly vulnerable to backdoor poisoning, yet existing defenses typically rely on rule-based approaches that lack semantic understanding, making them vulnerable to stealthy triggers and harmful to benign structures. To solve this, we present FedLSG, the first framework that integrates large language models (LLMs) into federated graph backdoor defense. FedLSG introduces a graph and behavior to text grounding scheme that transforms local graph structures and client update behaviors into semantically rich natural language representations. The framework further adopts a lightweight student-teacher architecture. On the server side, a full scale LLM serves as a teacher, providing global contextual guidance and evaluating client updates during aggregation to identify potentially malicious participants. On the client side, a LoRA-based student is maintained to perform semantic reasoning, to suppress the influence of edges associated with backdoor triggers. By enabling semantic interpretation of both graph patterns and client behaviors, the framework adaptively incorporates rule-based signals into message passing and client aggregation for defense. Experiments demonstrate that FedLSG significantly improves resistance to backdoor attacks without compromising graph integrity.

</details>


### [109] [GhostPrompt: Cross-Image Adversarial Prompt for Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19683)
*Li Zeng,Zeyu Ye,Meng Xie,Hangtao Zhang,Xianlong Wang,Yanchun Li,Zhetao Li*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出GhostPrompt，一种经单次优化即可跨图像复用的对抗性提示，通过联合优化提取图像不变对抗特征，在保持高攻击成功率的同时降低约70%的计算时间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有针对视觉-语言模型（VLM）的文本攻击大多从语言模型方法改编而来，优化过程中视觉输入固定，导致对抗性提示与特定图像绑定，跨图像迁移性差，限制了攻击效果。

Method: 提出GhostPrompt，采用联合优化方法：交替进行（1）为当前提示构造"最坏情况"的硬视觉条件；（2）更新提示使其在这些条件下保持有效，从而将图像不变对抗特征蒸馏到提示中。

Result: 在主流VLM上，相比最先进基线，攻击成功率提升超过30%，计算时间减少约70%。

Conclusion: GhostPrompt证明了跨图像可迁移对抗提示的有效性，为VLM对抗攻击提供了新视角，代码已开源。

Abstract: Vision-Language Models (VLMs) are known to be vulnerable to adversarial attacks, where subtle perturbations to images or texts induce erroneous outputs. However, most text-based attacks are adapted from language-model-centric methods, in which the visual input is fixed during optimization, resulting in adversarial prompts that are tied to specific images and thus limiting their attack effectiveness. To this end, we first introduce a new research perspective: cross-image transferability for adversarial prompts. We then propose GhostPrompt, an adversarial prompt that is optimized once and reused to steer VLM outputs toward attacker-specified responses across diverse images. GhostPrompt employs a joint optimization that distills image-invariant adversarial features into the prompt by "worst-case" generation. Specifically, it alternates between constructing hard visual conditions for the current prompt and updating the prompt to remain effective under these conditions. Extensive experiments on prevalent VLMs verify that \ourmethod achieves an improvement of over 30% in attack success rates compared to state-of-the-art (SoTA) baselines, while reducing computation time by ~70%. Our code is avalable at https://github.com/Ye-ze-yu/GhostPrompt.

</details>


### [110] [An Automated Framework for Extracting Reachable Attack Chains from Cyber Threat Intelligence Reports](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19742)
*Wenbo Hou,Ning Hu,Xueping Wang,Jiahao Gu,Wenjian Luo*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 提出一个自动化框架，利用大语言模型从CTI报告中提取可到达的攻击链，将每个攻击步骤建模为前置条件、攻击行为和后置条件的攻击单元，并编译为Datalog规则进行攻击目标可达性推理。在20份CTI报告（334个人工验证步骤）上，该方法在攻击行为恢复的覆盖率和攻击单元的完整性与一致性上优于现有系统，并在19份报告中成功推理出攻击目标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CTI报告包含丰富的攻击过程描述，但非结构化文本无法直接用于自动化攻击路径推理。现有CTI提取方法关注指标、实体或TTP标签，缺少对攻击步骤执行条件和结果状态的建模，不支持跨多阶段攻击链的状态匹配与可达性分析。

Method: 提出自动化框架，将每个攻击步骤建模为攻击单元（前置条件、攻击行为、后置条件）。采用多阶段LLM流水线：提取攻击行为骨架→恢复前置条件和后置条件→归一化为预定义谓词→修复断裂依赖→编译为Datalog规则进行攻击目标可达性推理。

Result: 在20份CTI报告（334个人工验证步骤）上，该方法在攻击行为恢复的覆盖率和攻击单元的完整性与一致性上优于代表性CTI提取系统和端到端LLM基线。19/20的报告通过Datalog推理达到指定攻击目标，向后搜索生成34条攻击路径。

Conclusion: 通过显式建模攻击步骤的前置/后置条件并编译为Datalog规则，该方法能有效从非结构化CTI报告中提取可推理的攻击链，支持自动化攻击路径可达性分析，显著优于现有提取方法。
